# BadaBhai — Manufacturing Trade Taxonomy and Question Pack v2

**From:** Akshit (CEO) · **To:** Prakash and team · **Date:** 23 Nov 2026
**Status:** RULED. Load it as written; bring back only the items in section 11.
**Answers:** questions Q1 to Q14 and standing decisions 1 to 7 of your brief of 23 Nov ("BadaBhai Campus into BadaBhai"). The rest of that brief (best profiles, company flow, matching rule, technical review) is answered separately.
**Replaces:** the trade list and the Phase 2 sheets of `BadaBhai_Launch_Plan_and_Taxonomy_Pack_v1.md` (2 Nov). The 16 live trade forms are not touched.

---

## 0. Read this first

1. **BadaBhai is not a CNC / VMC app.** CNC and VMC are where the marketing money goes first, because companies and workers are easiest to get there. The product must already understand every mechanical manufacturing job, so that any job or candidate from Campus has a trade to land in.
2. **The catalogue is now 60 trades in 11 departments** (section 3). 16 are live and stay exactly as they are. 7 thin worker-only forms are deepened to full depth. 37 trades are new.
3. **Engineer and desk roles are real trades now** (Production, Process, NPD, Quality, Maintenance, Service, PPC, Purchase, Stores, Dispatch, Safety, Project, Technical Sales, CAE, Piping). They are not labels. This changes your suggested default on decision 5.
4. **Every one of the 60 trades is postable by a company.** No worker-only trades remain.
5. **Civil, architecture, interior and BIM stay out.** A candidate keeps the role as a label on his profile; nothing matches on it.
6. **"Machine Operator" is not a trade.** It is a first question that sends the worker to the right machine trade (section 6). For old Campus jobs it maps to the existing general CNC Operator skill until an admin narrows it.
7. **Each sheet in section 9 is in the format of your Appendix A,** question by question, and has been machine-checked against your own rules (key format, one question mark, 20 words, six experience bands first, ladder second, a separating question per upper rung, chips, conditions, 18 to 23 questions).
8. **Not in this pack:** the Devanagari spoken twin of each question. Wording must be signed off first (section 11), otherwise the twin is recorded twice. Devanagari is given only for the worker phrases, which you need on day one.

---

## 1. Rulings on your questions

| Your question | Ruling |
|---|---|
| **Q1** Full list of trades | Section 3: 60 trades. Sources and confidence in section 12. |
| **Q2** A sheet per new trade | Section 9: 44 sheets (37 new, 7 deepened). |
| **Q3** Make the 7 worker-only trades postable, and deepen them | Yes to both. Deepened forms are T17, T24 (with T25 and T26), T27, T29, T38, T45 and T58. Where a live question asks the same thing, keep the live key and take the chips from this pack. Never rename a key. |
| **Q4** The four polymer trades | Blow Moulding (T30) and Rubber Moulding (T32) get sheets. **Plastic Process Technician** is not a trade: it is the Senior rung of Injection Moulding (T29), and the phrase leads there. **Mould / Die Maker** is not a new trade: it is the live Tool & Die Maker (T07), and the phrase leads there. Plastic Extrusion (T31) is added. |
| **Q5** Engineer and desk roles | Yes, trades with a form each. Ladder: Trainee (GET / DET) → Engineer → Senior Engineer → Lead / Asst. Manager → Manager / HOD. Stores and dispatch use Assistant → Executive → Senior Executive → In-charge. |
| **Q6** Design roles beyond 2D and 3D | **One trade,** 3D CAD Designer / Mechanical Design Engineer (T58), with a mandatory specialisation question (product, SPM and machine, sheet metal, plastic part, jig and fixture, press tool, mould, BIW, reverse engineering). CAE / FEA Analyst (T59) and Piping Designer (T60) are separate trades. HVAC design stays a label. |
| **Q7** "Machine Operator" | Not a trade. A routing question (section 6). |
| **Q8** ArtCAM Designer | Its own trade: CNC Router / Engraving Operator (T21), where the ArtCAM designer is the Programmer rung. Related to CAM Programmer. This changes your default on decision 4. |
| **Q9** CMM and NDT | Separate trades: CMM Operator / Programmer (T33), NDT Technician (T34). Also Calibration / Metrology Lab (T35) and Material Testing Lab (T36). All related to Quality Inspector. |
| **Q10** Civil, architecture, BIM, interior | Out of the taxonomy. Label only, no matching. |
| **Q11** SCADA, DCS, Instrumentation, Robotics | SCADA and DCS fold into PLC / Automation Technician (T38) as chips; its Senior Technician rung is shown as "Automation Engineer". Robotics (T39) and Instrumentation (T40) are separate trades. |
| **Q12** Related trades | Section 5 gives the complete list, including the trades that had none. |
| **Q13** Industry and domain above the trade | Two levels only: **Department → Trade** (section 2). Industry is a tag on the job and on the worker (the `sector_worked` answer), never a level of the tree. |
| **Q14** Who signs off wording | A practising worker or trainer of that trade. For trades RVM teaches: RVM's department head. For the rest: one practising worker found by the placement team. The CEO does not sign wording. Until a sheet is signed it can go live for chips and typing; hold only its voice recording. |

| Standing decision | Ruling |
|---|---|
| **1** 3D CAD / Mechanical Design postable | Yes (T58). |
| **2** PLC / Automation postable | Yes (T38). |
| **3** "Machine Operator" | As you suggested: old Campus jobs map to the general CNC Operator skill and an admin narrows them. New jobs must pick a machine trade. |
| **4** ArtCAM Designer | Own trade T21 (not a weak CAM match). |
| **5** Production, manufacturing and quality engineer roles | Trades now: T46, T47, T37 (and T43, T48, T52, T54). Not labels. |
| **6** Small domains | Supply chain, project and R&D now have trades (T52 to T55, T50, T48). Civil, BIM and architecture map to "none"; the candidate keeps the label. |
| **7** Who rules each row of the translation table | Section 7 is the ruling for every role named in your brief. Send the remaining role names in real use (you said about 70) as a list; they will be ruled in the same table. Divyanshu enters them. |

## 2. Structure: Department → Trade

| # | Department | What it holds |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Machining | CNC and conventional machine trades, EDM, gear cutting, boring, special machines, CNC router |
| 2 | Tool Room | Tool, die, mould, jig and fixture making |
| 3 | Sheet Metal & Fabrication | Sheet metal, press shop, welding, structural fabrication, tube forming |
| 4 | Casting, Forging & Surface Treatment | Foundry, die casting, forging, heat treatment, plating, painting |
| 5 | Plastics & Rubber | Injection, blow, extrusion, rubber moulding |
| 6 | Assembly | Fitting and line assembly |
| 7 | Quality & Metrology | Inspection, CMM, NDT, calibration, material lab, quality engineering |
| 8 | Maintenance & Automation | Mechanical and electrical maintenance, PLC, robots, instrumentation, HVAC, utilities, service |
| 9 | Production & Engineering | Supervision, production, process, NPD, safety, projects, technical sales |
| 10 | Planning & Supply Chain | PPC, stores, purchase, dispatch, forklift, crane |
| 11 | Design | 2D CAD, 3D CAD and mechanical design, CAE, piping |

Tool Room and Assembly hold only live trades; this pack adds trades in the other nine. CAM Programmer sits in Machining, next to the machines it programs.

**Kind.** Every trade is either *shop-floor* (ITI-type worker, fresher block asks about ITI workshop and trade test) or *desk* (diploma or degree holder, form asks qualification branch and internship).

**Level keys.** Use only these stored values. Four are new in this pack.

| Key | Label | New |
|---|---|---|
| `trainee` | Helper / Trainee (desk: GET / DET) | |
| `operator` | Operator | |
| `skilled` | Skilled | |
| `senior` | Senior | |
| `setter` | Setter | |
| `setter_programmer` | Setter-cum-Programmer | |
| `programmer` | Programmer | |
| `technician` | Technician | |
| `senior_technician` | Senior Technician | |
| `inspector` | Inspector | |
| `senior_inspector` | Senior Inspector | |
| `qc_engineer` | QC Engineer (Quality Inspector ladder only) | |
| `designer` | Designer / Draughtsman | |
| `senior_designer` | Senior Designer | |
| `lead` | Lead / Asst. Manager / Section head | |
| `supervisor` | Supervisor / In-charge / Head / Manager (top rung) | |
| `engineer` | Engineer / Officer | **new** |
| `senior_engineer` | Senior Engineer | **new** |
| `executive` | Executive (stores, dispatch) | **new** |
| `senior_executive` | Senior Executive | **new** |

**Contact price.** "Higher contact price from" on each sheet is the first rung that costs 2 credits when the worker also has 3 or more years, as the pricing brief says. Below that rung, and under 3 years, it is 1 credit.

## 4. Market view per trade

Size is MODELED (share of official factory and industry employment; low confidence). Demand and pay are ESTIMATE (minimum-wage floors plus spot checks of 2025–26 listings in NCR, Pune and Ahmedabad). Use them to order work, not to quote.

Rules that go with the list:

- **Legacy skills stay.** The live skill "Foundry / Forging Operator" is kept as a legacy label, related to Foundry / Casting (T24), Die Casting (T25) and Forging (T26). No new worker is given it. This is the same pattern as MIG / TIG / Arc Welder under Welder.
- **Safety / EHS Officer (T49) has no related trade.** It is a statutory role; nothing else substitutes for it.
- **A desk trade and its shop-floor trade are related only where a company really promotes across:** Production Supervisor ↔ Production Engineer, Quality Inspector ↔ Quality Engineer, Maintenance Technician ↔ Maintenance Engineer.
- **Nothing else is related.** Do not add "nearest" pairs.

## 6. First-question routing

These words are shared by several trades. They must not lead to any one form. The AI asks one routing question and then opens the right form.

| Worker says | Routing question | Chips → trade |
|---|---|---|
| machine operator, operator hoon, machine chalata hoon, production operator | "Aap kaunsi machine chalate hain?" | CNC lathe / turning → T01 · VMC / HMC / milling → T02 · Grinding → T03 · Manual lathe ya milling → T06 · Power press → T10 · Laser ya bending → T09 · Moulding machine → T29 · Die casting → T25 · Koi aur machine → ask free text |
| CNC operator, CNC ka kaam | "CNC me kaunsi machine chalate hain?" | Turning / lathe → T01 · VMC / HMC → T02 · Grinding → T03 · Wire cut / EDM → T17 · Router / engraving → T21 · Laser / bending / punch → T09 |
| helper, helper ka kaam | "Aap kis department me helper hain?" | Machine shop → machine trades question above · Welding / fabrication → T08 or T22 · Press shop → T10 · Moulding → T29 · Foundry / forging → T24 or T26 · Assembly → T15 · Store / dispatch → T53 or T55 · Maintenance → T13. Level is stored as `trainee`. |
| engineer hoon, diploma engineer, B.Tech hoon, GET, DET | "Aap kaunse department me kaam karte hain?" | Production → T46 · Quality → T37 · Maintenance → T43 · Process / ME / IE → T47 · NPD / development → T48 · PPC / planning → T52 · Purchase → T54 · Design → T58 · Service → T44 · Safety → T49 · Project / site → T50 · Sales → T51 |
| supervisor, in-charge, shift in-charge | "Aap kaunsa department sambhalte hain?" | One machine trade → that trade with level `supervisor` · A whole line or several trades → T45 · Stores → T53 · Quality → T16 or T37 · Maintenance → T13 or T43 |
| quality, QC, checking ka kaam | "Quality me aap kya karte hain?" | Line ya final inspection → T16 · CMM → T33 · NDT (UT, RT, MPT, DPT) → T34 · Calibration / standards room → T35 · Lab testing (spectro, hardness, UTM) → T36 · PPAP, audit, customer complaint → T37 |
| maintenance | "Maintenance me aap kya dekhte hain?" | Mechanical → T13 · Electrical → T14 · PLC / automation → T38 · Instruments → T40 · AC / chiller → T41 · Boiler / compressor / DG / ETP → T42 · Planning aur team (engineer) → T43 |
| designer, design ka kaam, CAD | "Aap kaunsa software chalate hain?" | Sirf AutoCAD 2D → T05 · SolidWorks / CATIA / NX / Creo → T58 · ANSYS / HyperMesh → T59 · PDMS / E3D / SP3D / Plant 3D → T60 · ArtCAM / Aspire → T21 · Mastercam / PowerMill → T04 |
| programmer | "Aap kis cheez ka program banate hain?" | CAM software se CNC program → T04 · Machine par hi program → T01 or T02 with level `programmer` · PLC → T38 · Robot → T39 · CMM → T33 · Laser / bending nesting → T09 |
| moulding | "Kaunsi moulding karte hain?" | Plastic injection → T29 · Blow → T30 · Rubber → T32 · Sand moulding (foundry) → T24 · Mould banata hoon (tool room) → T07 |
| die ka kaam, mould ka kaam | "Die ya mould banate hain, ya us par production karte hain?" | Banata / repair karta hoon → T07 · Design karta hoon → T58 · Press par production → T10 · Moulding machine par → T29 · Die casting machine par → T25 · Forging → T26 |
| welding aur fabrication | "Zyada kaam welding ka hai ya marking, cutting aur fit-up ka?" | Welding → T08 · Marking, cutting, fit-up → T22 · Patli sheet, laser, bending → T09 |
| fitter | "Aap kahaan fitting karte hain?" | Assembly, maintenance ya pipe fitting → T12 · Line par ek hi station → T15 · Structure / plate fit-up → T22 |
| electrician | "Aap kaunsa electrical kaam karte hain?" | Factory wiring, motor, panel → T14 · PLC program bhi → T38 · Instrument → T40 · AC / chiller → T41 |
| store, godown, warehouse | "Store me aapka kaam kya hai?" | Material receive, issue, entry → T53 · Packing aur dispatch → T55 · Forklift → T56 |
| driver, forklift, crane | "Aap kaunsi gaadi ya crane chalate hain?" | Forklift / reach truck / stacker → T56 · EOT / overhead / hydra crane → T57 · Truck ya tempo → not a manufacturing trade (label only) |
| lab, testing | "Lab me kaunsi testing karte hain?" | Spectro, hardness, UTM, microstructure → T36 · Calibration → T35 · NDT → T34 · CMM → T33 |

## 7. Campus to BadaBhai translation

### 7.1 Campus domain → BadaBhai department

| Campus domain | BadaBhai department |
|---|---|
| Design Mechanical | Design |
| Production | Machining, Tool Room, Sheet Metal & Fabrication, Casting, Forging & Surface Treatment, Plastics & Rubber, Assembly, Production & Engineering (by role, table below) |
| Quality | Quality & Metrology |
| Supply Chain | Planning & Supply Chain |

### 7.3 RVM course → trade (default, until the best-profile answers arrive)

## 8. How to load a trade, and in what order

**Per trade, in this order** (your own layers):

1. **Occupation codes.** Bind the family to the NCO-2015 codes on the sheet (the full list and the binding rules are in section 13). Three trades (CMM, NDT, Piping) have no occupation in NCO-2015: add one catalogue entry under the unit group named. One code belongs to one family only.
2. **Worker phrases.** Load every word on the three "Words" lines, Hinglish and Devanagari, pointing at the trade's occupation. Load the "must NOT lead here" words as routing entries (section 6), not as phrases of this trade.
3. **Profiling family and form.** Form id and family are on the sheet. Publish the questions exactly as written.
4. **Match skill.** One base skill per trade (the trade key). Add the related pairs from section 5.
5. **Answer-to-skill rows.** Only the chips listed on the sheet under "Chips that add a second match skill". Nothing else.
6. **Level table.** Ladder, confirmed rung and higher-price rung from the sheet.
7. **Posting catalogue.** Add the trade. The job form uses the same ladder and chips.
8. **Résumé map.** Headline pattern from the sheet; answers print in form order under Machines / Systems, Work done, Tools and Standards, Sectors, Training.
9. **Spoken twin.** Later, after the wording sign-off.

**Two things your loader should know**

- **Five questions are gated on a multi-select answer,** not on experience or level: `rubber_press_type` and `rubber_press_capacity` (T32), `design_tool_work` and `design_machine_elements` (T58), `cae_process_sim_software` (T59). Read "is one of" there as "any of these is selected". If the engine cannot do that, drop the condition on those five; each carries an escape chip, so nothing breaks.
- **Stored values never carry digits except pure number bands.** Names that contain a number are spelled out, as in your own forms (`three_axis`): for example `adc_twelve`, `r_four_ten_a`, `e_three_d`. Number bands (tonnage, capacity, team size) store the upper bound of the band as a plain number.

**Limits respected:** the longest form here is 23 questions. With the 17 common questions the conversation stays at or under 40, inside your cap of 48.

**Order of loading**

| Wave | What | Why first |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | The 7 deepened forms (T17, T24 with T25 and T26, T27, T29, T38, T45, T58), plus T21, T33, T37, T43, T46, T47, T48, T52, T54 | They give a home to the Campus jobs and students that match nothing today: CAD Designer, PLC, ArtCAM, production, quality, maintenance and planning engineers |
| 2 | The remaining shop-floor trades (T18, T19, T20, T22, T23, T28, T30, T31, T32, T34, T35, T36, T39, T40, T41, T42, T56, T57) | Volume trades outside RVM; needed for the non-RVM worker channels |
| 3 | T44, T49, T50, T51, T53, T55, T59, T60 | Complete the catalogue |

Marketing focus does not change with any wave: CNC Turner, VMC and the CAD / CAM trades.

---

## 9. Trade sheets

How to read a sheet: the header table carries what your level table, posting catalogue and family binding need. The three "Words" lines feed the worker-phrase layer. The questions are in the Appendix A line format.

## T17 · EDM / Wire-cut Operator

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `edm_wirecut_operator` |
| Department | Machining |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | DEEPEN (supersedes the live thin form) |
| Form id | `qp_edm_wirecut` |
| Profiling family | `fam_edm_wirecut` |
| Base match skill | `edm_wirecut_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 7223.2800 Die Copying Machine Operator/Die Sinking Electric Discharge Machine Operator. Unit group(s): 7223 Metal Working Machine Tool Setters and Operators. Partial gap: wire-cut EDM is not named. The only EDM title is 7223.2800 (die-sinking EDM). A new entry for Wire-cut EDM Operator is needed under 7223 |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Tool & Die Maker (Dies & Moulds), 2 years, NSQF 4; Tool & Die Maker (Press Tools, Jigs & Fixtures), 2 years, NSQF 4; Machinist, 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named for EDM; taken under Tool and Die Maker (Dies and Moulds) (2 years) or Machinist (2 years). NSQF job roles: CNC Setter and Operator - Electro Discharge Machine (Spark Erosion) (CGSSC, CSC/Q0121); Operator - Computer Numerically Controlled Electro Discharge Machine (Spark Erosion) (CGSSC, CSC/Q0118); Setter and Operator - Non-conventional Electro Discharge Machine (Spark Erosion) (CGSSC, CSC/Q0122). Also: Diploma in Tool & Die Making (4 years) at MSME Tool Rooms / NTTF / GTTC; MSME Technology Centres / Tool Rooms (IGTR Aurangabad, Ahmedabad, Indore; CTTC Bhubaneswar, Kolkata; CITD Hyderabad; CTR Ludhiana; IDTR Jamshedpur) certificate and diploma courses; Machine-maker training (Electronica and others) - general knowledge; On-the-job learning from helper level is the commonest real route; many skilled hands have no certificate |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹14,000–17,500 · Operator ₹17,000–25,000 · Setter ₹23,000–33,000 · Setter-cum-Programmer ₹28,000–42,000 · Supervisor ₹35,000–55,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Setter `setter` → Setter-cum-Programmer `setter_programmer` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `setter` |
| Higher contact price from | `setter` |
| Related trades | Tool & Die Maker (T07) |
| Typical employers | Tool rooms, mould and die makers, press tool shops, carbide die makers, precision job shops |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** wire cut, wirecut, wire cut operator, wire cut machine, wirecut ka kaam, wire edm, cnc wire cut, edm, edm operator, edm machine, edm ka kaam, cnc edm, spark erosion, spark machine, sparking machine, sparking ka kaam, die sinking, die sinker, sinker edm, znc, znc machine, znc operator, edm drill, super drill, electronica wire cut, sprintcut, sodick machine, robocut, moly wire cut, wayar cut
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** वायर कट, वायर कट मशीन, वायरकट ऑपरेटर, ईडीएम ऑपरेटर, ईडीएम मशीन, स्पार्क मशीन, स्पार्किंग मशीन, स्पार्क इरोज़न, जेडएनसी मशीन, डाई सिंकिंग
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "CNC operator" → ask which machine; "tool room" → ask which machine (wire cut, EDM, VMC ya grinder); "die ka kaam" → Tool & Die Maker; "die polish" → Tool & Die Maker; "sparking" with welding → Welder; "wire ka kaam" or "wiring" → Industrial Electrician; "laser cutting" → Sheet Metal Worker; "drill machine" → Conventional Machinist
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `EDM / Wire-cut Operator · {level} · {experience} · {edm_machine_type} · {edm_machine_make} · {city}`

### Questions (23)

1. **`edm_experience`** (tier: easy; pick one; mandatory)
   - Asks: "Wire cut ya EDM ka kitna tajurba hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Tajurbe ke hisaab se aage ke sawaal poochhe jaate hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Fresher → `0`; 1 saal se kam → `0`; 1 se 3 saal → `2`; 3 se 5 saal → `4`; 5 se 10 saal → `7`; 10 saal se zyada → `12`
2. **`edm_level`** (tier: easy; pick one; mandatory)
   - Asks: "Wire cut ya EDM machine pe aap kya karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Level ke hisaab se sahi sawaal poochhe jaate hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Helper/Trainee → `trainee`; Operator → `operator`; Setter → `setter`; Setter-cum-Programmer → `setter_programmer`; Supervisor → `supervisor`
3. **`edm_setter_confirm`** (tier: easy; pick one)
   - Asks: "Naye job ki clamping, dialling aur edge find aap khud karte ho?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Job khud set karne wale setter ko alag kaam milta hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Khud karta hoon → `self`; Koi aur karta hai → `other`
   - Asked only when: `edm_level` is one of setter
4. **`edm_setter_programmer_confirm`** (tier: easy; pick one)
   - Asks: "Drawing ya DXF se cutting program aap khud banate ho?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Khud program banane wale ko programmer ka kaam milta hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Khud banata hoon → `self`; Bana-banaya program chalata hoon → `other`
   - Asked only when: `edm_level` is one of setter_programmer
5. **`edm_team_confirm`** (tier: easy; free text)
   - Asks: "Kitni machine aur kitne log aapke under hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Team sambhalne wale supervisor ko alag zimmedari milti hai."
   - Asked only when: `edm_level` is one of supervisor
6. **`edm_machine_type`** (tier: medium; pick any; mandatory)
   - Asks: "Aap kaunsi EDM machine chalate hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Machine ke hisaab se sahi kaam dikhaya jaata hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): CNC wire cut (brass wire) → `wirecut`; Molybdenum / reusable wire cut → `moly_wirecut`; ZNC die-sinking EDM → `znc_sinker`; CNC die-sinking EDM → `cnc_sinker`; EDM drill / super drill → `edm_drill`; Koi aur machine → `unknown` (escape chip)
7. **`edm_machine_make`** (tier: medium; pick any; mandatory)
   - Asks: "Machine kis company ki hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Usi company ki machine wale employer aapko pehle dhoondhte hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Electronica (Sprintcut, Ultracut) → `electronica`; Sodick → `sodick`; Mitsubishi → `mitsubishi`; Makino → `makino`; Fanuc Robocut → `fanuc_robocut`; AgieCharmilles / GF → `agie_charmilles`; Ratnaparkhi → `ratnaparkhi`; Sparkonix → `sparkonix`; Koi aur company → `unknown` (escape chip)
8. **`edm_operation`** (tier: medium; pick any; core)
   - Asks: "Machine par kaunsa kaam karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Kaam ke naam se employer samajhta hai aap kya kar sakte hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Seedha profile cutting (punch, die, insert) → `profile_cut`; Taper cutting → `taper_cut`; Skim cut / multi-pass finishing → `skim_cut`; Top-bottom alag shape (4-axis) → `four_axis_cut`; Cavity sparking (die-sinking) → `cavity_sparking`; Rib, slot aur corner sparking → `rib_slot`; Start hole / chhota hole drilling → `start_hole`; Tooti hui tap ya drill nikalna → `tap_removal`
9. **`edm_job_type`** (tier: medium; pick any; core)
   - Asks: "Aap kaunse job banate hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Job ke hisaab se milti julti tool room dikhayi jaati hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Press tool ke punch aur die → `press_tool_parts`; Plastic mould ke core, cavity, insert → `mould_inserts`; Extrusion die → `extrusion_die`; Carbide die aur punch → `carbide_die`; Die casting ya forging die → `casting_forging_die`; Gear, spline ya keyway cutting → `gear_keyway`; Jig, fixture aur gauge → `jig_gauge`
10. **`material_worked`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Kaunse material par cutting ya sparking ki hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Hard material aur carbide ka tajurba alag mayne rakhta hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Hardened die steel (D2, HCHCr, OHNS) → `die_steel`; Hot die steel (H13, H11) → `hot_die_steel`; Mould steel (P20) → `mould_steel`; Carbide → `carbide`; MS, mild steel → `mild_steel`; EN series alloy steel → `alloy_steel`; Stainless steel → `stainless`; Aluminium → `aluminium`; Copper ya brass → `brass`
11. **`edm_wire_electrode`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Kaunsi wire ya electrode use karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Wire aur electrode se aapke kaam ki baariki pata chalti hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Brass wire 0.25 mm → `brass_wire`; Zinc coated wire → `coated_wire`; Patli wire, 0.20 mm ya usse kam → `fine_wire`; Molybdenum wire → `moly_wire`; Copper electrode → `copper_electrode`; Graphite electrode → `graphite_electrode`; Copper tungsten electrode → `copper_tungsten`; Brass ya copper tube (EDM drill) → `tube_electrode`
12. **`measuring_tools`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Kaunse measuring instrument chala lete hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Naap lene wale operator ko fine kaam diya jaata hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Vernier caliper → `vernier`; Micrometer → `micrometer`; Dial indicator / lever dial → `dial_indicator`; Height gauge → `height_gauge`; Pin gauge → `pin_gauge`; Slip gauge → `slip_gauge`; Profile projector → `profile_projector`; Bore dial gauge → `bore_gauge`
13. **`drawing_reading`** (tier: advanced; pick one)
   - Asks: "Kya aap drawing padh lete hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Drawing padhna kai jagah zaroori hota hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Nahi padh pata → `none`; Basic 2D drawing padh leta hoon → `basic_drawing`; GD aur T bhi samajhta hoon → `gdt`
14. **`edm_setting_work`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Setting mein aap kya kya karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Setting karne wale ko operator se zyada paisa milta hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Job clamping aur dialling → `clamping_dialling`; Wire vertical alignment → `wire_alignment`; Edge find aur centre find → `edge_centre_find`; Wire threading, auto ya haath se → `wire_threading`; Electrode centring aur depth set karna → `electrode_setting`; Flushing nozzle aur pressure set karna → `flushing_setting`; Wire guide aur power contact badalna → `guide_contact_change`; Pehla piece set karna → `first_piece`
   - Asked only when: `edm_experience` is at least 2 OR `edm_level` is one of setter, setter_programmer, supervisor
15. **`edm_parameter_work`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Cutting parameter mein aap kya set karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Parameter samajhne wale ko mushkil job diye jaate hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Technology table se condition chunna → `tech_table`; Pulse on / off time → `pulse_on_off`; Peak current (IP) → `peak_current`; Servo voltage / gap voltage → `servo_voltage`; Wire tension aur wire feed → `wire_tension_feed`; Skim cut ke liye wire offset → `wire_offset`; Electrode undersize / spark gap ka hisaab → `spark_gap`; Parameter koi aur set karta hai → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `edm_experience` is at least 2 OR `edm_level` is one of setter, setter_programmer, supervisor
16. **`tolerance_band`** (tier: advanced; pick one)
   - Asks: "Aap kitni tolerance tak kaam kar lete hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Tolerance se aapke kaam ki fine quality pata chalti hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): 0.10 mm tak → `0.10`; 0.05 mm tak → `0.05`; 0.02 mm tak → `0.02`; 0.01 mm tak → `0.01`; 0.005 mm (5 micron) tak → `0.005`; Pata nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `edm_experience` is at least 2 OR `edm_level` is one of setter, setter_programmer, supervisor
17. **`sector_worked`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Kis line ke liye kaam kiya hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Sector ka tajurba usi line ke employer dhoondhte hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Tool room, die aur mould → `die_mould`; Automotive ya auto parts → `automotive`; General engineering, job shop → `general_engg`; Defence ya aerospace → `defence`; Electronics, connector, motor lamination → `electronics`; Medical ya surgical parts → `medical`; Fastener ke die → `fasteners`; Inme se koi nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `edm_experience` is at least 2 OR `edm_level` is one of setter, setter_programmer, supervisor
18. **`edm_programming_level`** (tier: advanced; pick one)
   - Asks: "Program ke saath aap kitna kaam karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Program banane wale ko sabse zyada paisa milta hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Sirf program load karke chalata hoon → `load_run`; Offset, taper aur condition badal leta hoon → `edit_program`; Machine par hi profile bana kar program nikalta hoon → `machine_cam`; Computer par CAM software se banata hoon → `cam`
   - Asked only when: `edm_experience` is at least 2 OR `edm_level` is one of setter, setter_programmer, supervisor
19. **`edm_cam_software`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Program kis software se banate hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Software jaanne wale programmer ki tool room me maang hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): ELCAM → `elcam`; Heart NC (Sodick) → `heart_nc`; Machine ka apna onboard CAM → `onboard_cam`; AutoCAD se DXF bana kar → `autocad_dxf`; Mastercam Wire → `mastercam_wire`; AutoCut (reusable wire machine) → `autocut`; Koi aur software → `unknown` (escape chip)

20. **`troubleshooting`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Machine ki kaunsi dikkat aap sudhaar lete hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Dikkat sudhaarne wale operator ki shop par zaroorat rehti hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Wire baar baar tootna → `wire_break`; Size ya taper ka farak → `size_variation`; Surface par line ya mark aana → `surface_finish`; Short circuit ya arcing → `short_circuit`; Electrode jaldi ghisna → `electrode_wear`; Flushing kam, cutting slow hona → `poor_flushing`; Filter, resin ya dielectric badalna → `filter_resin`; Inme se koi nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)

21. **`iti_workshop_machines`** (tier: easy; pick any; core)
   - Asks: "ITI ya training me kaunsi machine par kaam kiya hai?"

## T19 · Boring Machine Operator (HBM)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `boring_machine_operator` |
| Department | Machining |

| Status | NEW |

| Base match skill | `boring_machine_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 7222.0400 Jig Borer; 7223.1701 Borer/Operator Boring Machine Operator; 7223.1800 Cylinder Borer. Unit group(s): 7223 Metal Working Machine Tool Setters and Operators; 7222 Tool Makers and Related Workers |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Machinist, 2 years, NSQF 4; Turner, 2 years, NSQF 4; Operator Advanced Machine Tool, 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named for boring machines; taken under Machinist (2 years). NSQF job roles: Operator - Boring Machine (CGSSC, CSC/Q0107). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); On-the-job learning from helper level is the commonest real route; many skilled hands have no certificate; Heavy-engineering PSU and large-company apprenticeship (general knowledge) |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹14,000–17,500 · Operator ₹18,000–27,000 · Setter ₹25,000–38,000 · Setter-cum-Programmer ₹32,000–48,000 · Supervisor ₹38,000–58,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Setter `setter` → Setter-cum-Programmer `setter_programmer` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `setter` |
| Higher contact price from | `setter` |
| Related trades | VMC / Milling Operator (T02); Conventional Machinist (T06) |
| Typical employers | Heavy engineering and machine-building shops, gearbox and pump makers, press and machine-tool builders, heavy job-work machine shops |

## T22 · Fabricator / Structural Fitter

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `fabricator` |
| Department | Sheet Metal & Fabrication |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_fabrication` |
| Profiling family | `fam_fabrication` |
| Base match skill | `fabricator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 7212.0400 Gas Cutter; 7212.0402 Plasma Cutter – Manual; 7214.0500 Boiler Maker; 7214.0600 Erector, Structural; 7214.0700 Fitter, Structural; 7214.0900 Riveter; 7214.9900 Structural Metal Prepares and Erectors, Other; 7222.0100 Marker, Metal; 7224.0102 Fitter – Fabrication; 7224.0401 Grinder – Hand-Held and Power Tools. Unit group(s): 7214 Structural Metal Preparers and Erectors; 7212 Welders and Flame Cutters; 7222 Tool Makers and Related Workers; 7224 Metal Polishers, Wheel Grinders and Tool Sharpeners |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Welder (Fabrication & Fitting), 1 year, NSQF 2.5; Fitter, 2 years, NSQF 4; Sheet Metal Worker, 1 year, NSQF 2.5; Welder (Structural), 1 year, NSQF 2.5. Apprenticeship: Designated trades: Technician Fabricator (2 years); Gas Cutter (2 years, ship-building group); Structural Welder (1 year 3 months). NSQF job roles: Fitter - Fabrication (CGSSC, CSC/Q0303); Oxy Fuel Gas Cutter (CGSSC, CSC/Q0203); Plasma Cutter - Manual (CGSSC, CSC/Q0207); Fabrication and Service Technician (ASDC, ASC/Q6422). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); On-the-job learning from helper level is the commonest real route; many skilled hands have no certificate; IBR / ASME shop experience counts more than any certificate |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹13,500–16,500 · Fabricator ₹17,000–24,000 · Skilled ₹22,000–33,000 · Senior ₹30,000–45,000 · Supervisor ₹35,000–55,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Fabricator `operator` → Skilled `skilled` → Senior `senior` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `skilled` |
| Higher contact price from | `skilled` |
| Related trades | Welder (T08); Sheet Metal Worker (T09); Fitter (T12) |
| Typical employers | Heavy and structural fabrication shops, pressure vessel and tank makers (IBR / ASME shops), PEB and steel structure companies, machine frame and chassis makers, project site contractors |

## T25 · Die Casting Operator (PDC / GDC)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `die_casting_operator` |
| Department | Casting, Forging & Surface Treatment |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_die_casting` |
| Profiling family | `fam_die_casting` |
| Base match skill | `die_casting_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 8121.4200 Die Casting Machine Operator; 8121.4201 Pressure Die Casting (PDC) Operator. Unit group(s): 8121 Metal Processing Plant Operators |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Foundryman, 1 year, NSQF 3.5; Tool & Die Maker (Dies & Moulds), 2 years, NSQF 4 (for die setters); Fitter, 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named for pressure die casting; Foundryman (2 years) is the nearest. NSQF job roles: Casting Technician Level 3 (ASDC, ASC/Q3202). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); On-the-job learning from helper level is the commonest real route; many skilled hands have no certificate; Machine-maker training - general knowledge |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹13,500–16,500 · Operator ₹16,000–22,000 · Setter ₹21,000–30,000 · Process Technician ₹27,000–40,000 · Supervisor ₹32,000–50,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Setter `setter` → Senior `senior` (shown as "Process Technician") → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `setter` |
| Higher contact price from | `setter` |
| Related trades | Foundry / Casting Operator (T24); Foundry / Forging Operator (legacy live skill) |
| Typical employers | Aluminium die-casting suppliers to two-wheeler and car makers, alloy wheel plants, zinc die-casting units, fan and LED housing makers |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** die casting, die casting operator, diecasting, dai casting, pdc, pdc operator, pdc machine, pdc setter, pdc shop, hpdc, hpdc operator, pressure die casting, gdc, gdc operator, gravity die casting, lpdc, low pressure die casting, aluminium die casting, aluminium casting machine, zinc die casting, hot chamber machine, cold chamber machine, die casting setter, die casting machine operator, toshiba die casting, buhler die casting, alloy wheel casting
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** डाई कास्टिंग, डाई कास्टिंग ऑपरेटर, पीडीसी ऑपरेटर, पीडीसी मशीन, जीडीसी, प्रेशर डाई कास्टिंग, ग्रेविटी डाई कास्टिंग, एल्युमिनियम डाई कास्टिंग
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "casting" → ask die casting ya sand casting (Foundry / Casting Operator); "moulding machine" → ask plastic (Injection Moulding Operator) ya die casting; "die ka kaam", "die fitter" → Tool & Die Maker; "die setter" → ask press shop, forging ya die casting; "foundry", "dhalai" → Foundry / Casting Operator; "trimming" → ask die casting trimming ya Press Operator; "furnace operator" → ask melting, holding furnace ya heat treatment
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `Die Casting Operator (PDC / GDC) · {level} · {experience} · {diecast_process} · {diecast_machine_brand} · {diecast_tonnage} · {city}`

# Department 5: Plastics & Rubber

## T29 · Injection Moulding Operator

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `injection_moulding_operator` |
| Department | Plastics & Rubber |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | DEEPEN (supersedes the live thin form; the name "Plastic Process Technician" becomes the Senior rung of this trade) |
| Form id | `qp_injection_moulding` |
| Profiling family | `fam_injection_moulding` |
| Base match skill | `injection_moulding_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 8142.0501 Injection Moulding Machine Operator (Plastic); 8142.0502 Moulding Operator; 8142.1300 Mould Setter (Plastic); 8142.1301 Plastic Moulding Technician or Operator. Unit group(s): 8142 Plastic Products Machine Operators |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Plastic Processing Operator, 1 year, NSQF 3.5. Apprenticeship: Designated trades: Injection Moulding Machine Operator (2 years); Plastic Processing Operator (2 years). NSQF job roles: Machine Operator - Injection Moulding (CIPET, CPC/Q0204); Machine Operator Assistant - Injection Moulding (CIPET, CPC/Q0203); Automotive Plastic Moulding Technician (ASDC, ASC/Q4401); Injection Moulding Operator (rubber) (Rubber, Chemical & Petrochemical Skill Development Council, RSC/Q0207). Also: CIPET (Central Institute of Petrochemicals Engineering & Technology): DPT (Diploma in Plastics Technology), DPMT (Diploma in Plastics Mould Technology), PGD-PPT and short NSQF operator courses; Diploma in Plastics Engineering / Polymer; Machine-maker training (Milacron, Haitian, Toshiba, Engel) - general knowledge; On-the-job learning from helper level is the commonest real route; many skilled hands have no certificate |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹13,500–16,000 · Operator ₹15,000–20,000 · Setter ₹20,000–30,000 · Process Technician ₹27,000–42,000 · Supervisor ₹32,000–55,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Setter `setter` → Senior `senior` (shown as "Process Technician") → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `setter` |
| Higher contact price from | `setter` |
| Related trades | Blow Moulding Operator (T30); Plastic Extrusion Operator (T31); Rubber Moulding Operator (T32) |
| Typical employers | Auto plastic part makers (Tier-1 and Tier-2), appliance and electrical part moulders, packaging and closure plants, custom moulding job shops |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** injection moulding, injection molding, moulding operator, molding operator, moulding machine operator, plastic moulding, plastic molding machine, moulding ka kaam, imm operator, injection machine, mould setter, mold setter, moulding setter, process technician, plastic process technician, mould change ka kaam, mould loading, plastic machine operator, ferromatik, milacron machine, windsor moulding machine, haitian machine, toshiba moulding machine, l&t moulding machine, engel machine, injecton molding, moldig machine

## T30 · Blow Moulding Operator

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `blow_moulding_operator` |
| Department | Plastics & Rubber |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_blow_moulding` |
| Profiling family | `fam_blow_moulding` |
| Base match skill | `blow_moulding_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 8142.0700 Bottle Blowing Machine Operator (Plastic). Unit group(s): 8142 Plastic Products Machine Operators |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Plastic Processing Operator, 1 year, NSQF 3.5. Apprenticeship: Designated trade: Blow Moulding Machine Operator (2 years). NSQF job roles: Machine Operator - Blow Moulding (CIPET, CPC/Q0404); Machine Operator Assistant - Blow Moulding (CIPET, CPC/Q0403). Also: CIPET (Central Institute of Petrochemicals Engineering & Technology): DPT (Diploma in Plastics Technology), DPMT (Diploma in Plastics Mould Technology), PGD-PPT and short NSQF operator courses; On-the-job learning from helper level is the commonest real route; many skilled hands have no certificate |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹13,500–16,000 · Operator ₹15,000–20,000 · Setter ₹19,000–28,000 · Senior ₹25,000–36,000 · Supervisor ₹30,000–48,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Setter `setter` → Senior `senior` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `setter` |
| Higher contact price from | `setter` |
| Related trades | Injection Moulding Operator (T29); Plastic Extrusion Operator (T31) |
| Typical employers | PET bottle and preform plants, HDPE bottle and container makers for FMCG, pharma and lube oil, jerry can, drum and tank makers, auto duct and reservoir moulders |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** blow moulding, blow molding, blow moulding operator, blow machine operator, blow molding ka kaam, bottle machine, bottle banane ki machine, pet blowing, pet blow machine, pet bottle plant, stretch blow machine, isbm machine, ebm machine, extrusion blow moulding, jerry can machine, drum blow moulding, tank blow moulding, parison, blow moulding setter, jagmohan machine, cmp machine, sipa machine, sidel machine, asb machine, kautex, bottle plant operator, blowing operator, blow mold, hdpe bottle machine
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** ब्लो मोल्डिंग, ब्लो मशीन ऑपरेटर, बोतल मशीन, पेट ब्लोइंग, पेट बोतल प्लांट, जेरी कैन मशीन, ड्रम मशीन, बोतल प्लांट
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "blown film" / "film plant" → Plastic Extrusion Operator; "moulding" alone → ask plastic injection, blow, rubber or foundry sand moulding; "preform machine" → ask preform injection (Injection Moulding Operator) or preform blowing (here); "bottle filling" / "filling line" → not this trade, ask whether he makes the bottle or fills it; "plastic tank" → ask blow moulding or roto moulding; "blower" → ask, an air blower or AC blower is not this trade
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None

|---|---|

| Base match skill | `cmm_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | No occupation in NCO-2015: a new catalogue entry is needed. Unit group(s): 7543 Product Graders and Testers (Excluding Food and Beverages). No CMM operator or programmer occupation in NCO-2015. File under 7543 (nearest existing titles: 7543.2001 Quality Inspector - Forged, Casted or Machined Components; 7543.3002 Quality Control Inspector - Dimension Check). A new catalogue entry is needed |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Machinist, 2 years, NSQF 4; Draughtsman Mechanical, 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named for CMM; Quality Assurance Assistant (2 years) is the nearest. Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); CMM-maker training: Zeiss (Calypso), Hexagon (PC-DMIS), Mitutoyo (MCOSMOS) - the certificates employers actually ask for; Metrology courses at MSME Technology Centres; GD&T courses (private) |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹15,000–19,000 · Operator ₹19,000–28,000 · Programmer ₹27,000–42,000 · Senior ₹38,000–58,000 · Supervisor ₹45,000–70,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Programmer `programmer` → Senior `senior` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `programmer` |
| Higher contact price from | `programmer` |
| Related trades | Quality Inspector (T16); Calibration / Metrology Lab Technician (T35) |
| Typical employers | Auto component plants (Tier-1 and Tier-2), precision machine shops, die casting and sheet metal plants, tool rooms, third-party CMM inspection labs |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** cmm, cmm operator, cmm programmer, cmm ka kaam, cmm machine, cmm inspector, cmm engineer, cmm room, cmm report, cmm oprator, c m m, coordinate measuring machine, zeiss cmm, calypso, pc dmis, pcdmis, mcosmos, mitutoyo cmm, hexagon cmm, accurate cmm, faro arm, vmm, vmm operator, vision measuring machine, 3d scanner operator, 3d scanning, layout inspection, cmm programming
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** सीएमएम, सीएमएम ऑपरेटर, सीएमएम प्रोग्रामर, सीएमएम मशीन, सीएमएम का काम, वीएमएम ऑपरेटर, ज़ाइस सीएमएम, कैलिप्सो, 3डी स्कैनर
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "quality" / "QC ka kaam" → ask whether inspection by gauges (Quality Inspector) or CMM; "inspector" / "inspection" → Quality Inspector unless CMM or VMM is named; "CNC programmer" → ask which machine (CNC Turner, VMC / Milling Operator, CAM Programmer); "calibration" → Calibration / Metrology Lab Technician; "metrology" alone → ask CMM room or calibration lab; "scanning" alone → ask whether 3D scanning of parts; "profile projector" alone → Quality Inspector
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `CMM Operator / Programmer · {level} · {experience} · {cmm_make} · {cmm_software} · {city}`

## T36 · Material Testing / Met Lab Technician

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `material_lab_technician` |
| Department | Quality & Metrology |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_material_lab` |
| Profiling family | `fam_material_lab` |
| Base match skill | `material_lab_technician` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 1223.0102 Manager Material Testing; 3117.0300 Laboratory Assistant, Metallurgical; 3119.0100 Laboratory Assistant, Engineering; 8122.6000 Sample Checker, Metal Making; 8122.6900 Hardness Tester, Metal. Unit group(s): 3117 Mining and Metallurgical Technicians; 3119 Physical and Engineering Science Technicians Not Elsewhere Classified; 8122 Metal Finishing, Plating and Coating Machine Operators; 1223 Research and Development Managers |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Laboratory Assistant (Chemical Plant), 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: Designated trades: Laboratory Assistant (Chemical Plant) (2 years); Chemical Laboratory Assistant (2 years). NSQF job roles: Lab Technician - Metal Testing (CGSSC, CSC/Q0602); Testing & QC for Plastics Materials & Products - Technician (CIPET, CPC/Q8103). Also: Diploma / B.Tech Metallurgy; B.Sc. Chemistry; CIPET testing courses for polymer labs; NABL lab exposure (ISO/IEC 17025) |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹14,000–18,000 · Technician ₹18,000–26,000 · Senior Technician ₹25,000–38,000 · Lab In-charge ₹38,000–60,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Technician `technician` → Senior Technician `senior_technician` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_technician` |
| Higher contact price from | `senior_technician` |
| Related trades | Heat-Treatment Technician (T27); NDT Technician (T34); Quality Inspector (T16) |
| Typical employers | Foundries, forging plants, heat treatment shops, steel and rolling mills, fastener and auto component plants, plastic and rubber part makers, NABL accredited testing labs |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** material testing, material testing lab, material lab, met lab, metallurgy lab, metallurgical lab, metallurgist, lab chemist foundry, spectro operator, spectro ka kaam, spectro lab, spectrometer operator, hardness testing, hardness checker, utm operator, tensile test, tensile testing, microstructure, metallography, physical lab, mechanical testing lab, chemical lab foundry, wet analysis, sand testing, sand lab, salt spray test, testing lab incharge, lab technician metallurgy
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** मटेरियल टेस्टिंग, मेट लैब, मेटलर्जी लैब, स्पेक्ट्रो ऑपरेटर, हार्डनेस टेस्टिंग, टेंसाइल टेस्ट, माइक्रोस्ट्रक्चर, सैंड टेस्टिंग, मटेरियल लैब टेक्नीशियन
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "lab technician" alone → ask which lab (material testing or calibration, pathology is not this trade); "chemist" alone → ask which plant (foundry lab comes here, pharma and plating do not); "testing" alone → ask which testing (material lab, NDT, electrical); "NDT" / "UT testing" / "DP test" → NDT Technician; "hardening" / "furnace operator" → Heat-Treatment Technician; "calibration lab" → Calibration / Metrology Lab Technician; "quality inspector" → Quality Inspector; "QC chemist pharma" → not this trade
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `Material Testing Lab Technician · {level} · {experience} · {matlab_test_type} · {material_worked} · {city}`

## T38 · PLC / Automation Technician

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `plc_automation_technician` |
| Department | Maintenance & Automation |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | DEEPEN (supersedes the live thin form) |
| Form id | `qp_plc_automation` |
| Profiling family | `fam_plc_automation` |
| Base match skill | `plc_automation_technician` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 7412.0101 Automation Specialist. Unit group(s): 7412 Electrical Mechanics and Fitters; 3113 Electrical Engineering Technicians; 3139 Process Control Technicians Not Elsewhere Classified. Partial gap: no PLC programmer or automation technician title. 7412.0101 Automation Specialist is the only automation title. File new entries under 3113 or 3139. Do not use 8212.2002 PLC Assembly Operator (assembles PLC hardware) |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Technician Mechatronics, 2 years, NSQF 4; Manufacturing Process Control and Automation Technician, 1 year, NSQF 3.5; Electrician, 2 years, NSQF 4; Instrument Mechanic, 2 years, NSQF 4; Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) Technician, 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: Designated trades: Operator PLC System (2 years); PLC Operator (2 years); Mechanic (Embedded Systems and PLC) (2 years); Mechanic Mechatronics (2 years); Mechanic Electrical Maintenance (Industrial Automation) (2 years). NSQF job roles: Industrial Automation Technician (Instrumentation Automation Surveillance & Communication Sector Skill Council); Industrial Automation Specialist (IASC); PLC Technician (Advanced) (IISSSC); Manufacturing and Mechatronics Lead Technician (ASDC, ASC/Q6424). Also: Diploma / B.Tech in Electrical, Electronics, Instrumentation or Mechatronics; Private PLC-SCADA institutes (3–6 month courses; quality varies widely); Siemens / Rockwell / Mitsubishi authorised training; MSME Technology Centres and NTTF mechatronics programmes |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹15,000–20,000 · Technician ₹20,000–32,000 · Senior Technician (Automation Engineer) ₹32,000–55,000 · Lead ₹50,000–80,000 · Supervisor / Head ₹70,000–1,10,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Technician `technician` → Senior Technician `senior_technician` (shown as "Automation Engineer") → Lead `lead` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_technician` |
| Higher contact price from | `senior_technician` |
| Related trades | Industrial Electrician (T14); Instrumentation Technician (T40); Robot Operator / Programmer (T39) |
| Typical employers | SPM and machine builders, system integrators and panel builders, maintenance departments of auto, FMCG, pharma and packaging plants |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** plc, plc ka kaam, plc programmer, plc programming, plc technician, plc engineer, automation engineer, automation technician, plc scada, scada, hmi programming, ladder logic, ladder program, siemens plc, s7 300, tia portal, allen bradley, ab plc, rslogix, mitsubishi plc, gx works, delta plc, omron plc, vfd drive ka kaam, servo drive, spm automation, plc commissioning, plc mentenance, plc programer, autometion engineer
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** पीएलसी, पीएलसी प्रोग्रामर, पीएलसी टेक्नीशियन, ऑटोमेशन इंजीनियर, स्काडा, एचएमआई, लैडर लॉजिक, वीएफडी ड्राइव, सीमेंस पीएलसी
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "automation" → ask PLC ka kaam hai ya robot ka; "panel wiring" → Industrial Electrician, unless he also says PLC; "electrician" → Industrial Electrician; "robot programmer" → Robot Operator / Programmer; "instrument ka kaam" → Instrumentation Technician; "CNC programmer" → ask which machine (CNC Turner or VMC / Milling Operator); "maintenance" → ask mechanical ya electrical (Maintenance Technician)

## T39 · Robot Operator / Programmer

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `robot_operator_programmer` |
| Department | Maintenance & Automation |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_robotics` |
| Profiling family | `fam_robotics` |
| Base match skill | `robot_operator_programmer` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 3139.1400 Robotic Machine Operator; 3139.9900 Industrial Robot Operators, Other. Unit group(s): 3139 Process Control Technicians Not Elsewhere Classified. Partial gap: no robot programmer title; file a new entry under 3139 |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Industrial Robotics & Digital Manufacturing Technician, 1 year, NSQF 3.5; Technician Mechatronics, 2 years, NSQF 4; Manufacturing Process Control and Automation Technician, 1 year, NSQF 3.5. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named for robots on the schedule read; Mechanic Mechatronics (2 years) is the nearest. NSQF job roles: Automotive Welding Machine Operator (Manual and Robotics) (ASDC, ASC/Q3102); Automotive Automation and Robotics Engineer (ASDC, ASC/Q8303); Industrial Robotic System Integration (NOS-based course) (ASDC, ASC/N8352). Also: Robot-maker training: Fanuc, Yaskawa, ABB, KUKA India academies (the certificates employers ask for) - general knowledge; Diploma / B.Tech Mechatronics, Electrical or Mechanical; MSME Technology Centres and private robotics institutes |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹15,000–19,000 · Operator ₹18,000–26,000 · Programmer ₹28,000–45,000 · Senior ₹42,000–65,000 · Supervisor ₹55,000–85,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Programmer `programmer` → Senior `senior` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `programmer` |
| Higher contact price from | `programmer` |
| Related trades | PLC / Automation Technician (T38) |
| Typical employers | Car and two-wheeler plants and their weld shops, Tier-1 welding and seating vendors, robot system integrators, die casting, moulding and packaging plants |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** robot operator, robot programmer, robot ka kaam, robotic welding, robot welding, welding robot, robot teaching, robot teacher, teach pendant, fanuc robot, yaskawa robot, motoman, abb robot, kuka robot, kawasaki robot, nachi robot, panasonic robot, spot welding robot, mig robot, robot cell, robot touch up, cobot, palletizer robot, pick and place robot, roboguide, robotstudio, robo operator, robot programer, robotic operator
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** रोबोट ऑपरेटर, रोबोट प्रोग्रामर, रोबोट वेल्डिंग, रोबोटिक वेल्डिंग, रोबोट टीचिंग, टीच पेंडेंट, फानुक रोबोट, रोबोट सेल
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "welder" or "mig welder" → Welder, unless he says robot; "spot welding operator" → Welder, ask haath ki gun hai ya robot; "PLC programmer" → PLC / Automation Technician; "automation" → ask PLC ka kaam hai ya robot ka; "CNC programmer" → ask which machine (CNC Turner or VMC / Milling Operator); "fanuc" → ask CNC controller hai ya robot; "moulding machine ka robot" → Injection Moulding Operator, unless he teaches the robot himself
**Chips that add a second match skill:** `robot_plc_interface` = `plc_programming` ("PLC ladder program bhi khud banata hoon") → PLC / Automation Technician (`plc_automation_technician`)
**Résumé headline:** `Robot Operator / Programmer · {level} · {experience} · {robot_brand} · {robot_application} · {robot_program_work} · {city}`

| Training route | ITI (CTS): Refrigeration and Air Conditioning Technician, 2 years, NSQF 4; Central Aircondition Plant Mechanic, 2 years, NSQF 4. ## T42 · Utility Operator (Boiler / Compressor / DG / ETP)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `utility_operator` |
| Department | Maintenance & Automation |
| Kind | Shop-floor |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_utility` |
| Profiling family | `fam_utility` |
| Base match skill | `utility_operator` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 3122.1600 Boiler Supervisor; 3131.0200 Stationary Engine Driver, Internal Combustion; 3132.0300 Gas-Compressor Operator; 3132.0400 Wastewater Treatment Plant Operator; 3132.0700 Air Compressor Operator; 3132.0800 Ammonia Compressor Attendant; 3132.1000 Pumpman; 8182.0100 Boiler, Tindal; 8182.0200 Boiler, Attendant; 8182.0300 Boiler, Fireman; 8182.0400 Boiler House Operatives. Unit group(s): 8182 Steam Engine and Boiler Operators; 3132 Incinerator and Water Treatment Plant Operators; 3131 Power Production Plant Operators; 3122 Manufacturing Supervisors |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Attendant Operator (Chemical Plant), 2 years, NSQF 4; Fitter, 2 years, NSQF 4; Electrician, 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: Designated trades: Boiler Attendant (2 years); Utility Operator (1 year 3 months); Operator Cum Mechanic Pollution Control Equipment (2 years); Steam Turbine-cum-Auxiliary Plant Operator (2 years). Also: Boiler Attendant certificate of competency, 2nd class and 1st class, from the State Boiler Examination Board (Boiler Attendants' Rules 2011) - statutory for running a boiler; general knowledge, not re-verified this session; Boiler Operation Engineer (BOE) certificate (Boiler Operation Engineers' Rules 2011) for large boilers / in-charge role; NPTI (National Power Training Institute) courses for power-plant operation; State pollution-board / supplier training for ETP-STP operators (no statutory licence known); DG and compressor makers' operator training |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹13,500–16,500 · Operator ₹16,000–22,000 · Licensed / Certified Operator (2nd / 1st class boiler attendant) ₹22,000–35,000 · Senior ₹30,000–42,000 · Supervisor (BOE / utility in-charge) ₹40,000–70,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Operator `operator` → Skilled `skilled` (shown as "Licensed / Certified Operator") → Senior `senior` → Supervisor `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `skilled` |
| Higher contact price from | `skilled` |
| Related trades | Refrigeration & AC (HVAC) Technician (T41); Maintenance Technician (T13) |
| Typical employers | Utility departments of pharma, chemical, textile, food, paper and auto plants; hospitals and hotels; O&M and facility management contractors |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** boiler operator, boiler attendant, boiler man, boiler ka kaam, boiler fireman, first class boiler attendant, second class boiler attendant, boe, utility operator, utility ka kaam, compressor operator, dg operator, dg set operator, generator operator, etp operator, stp operator, wtp operator, ro plant operator, dm plant operator, water treatment plant, pump operator, pump house operator, thermopack operator, thermic fluid heater, cooling tower operator, boilar operator, boyler operator, utillity operator
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** बॉयलर ऑपरेटर, बॉयलर अटेंडेंट, बॉयलर फायरमैन, यूटिलिटी ऑपरेटर, कंप्रेसर ऑपरेटर, डीजी ऑपरेटर, जनरेटर ऑपरेटर, ईटीपी ऑपरेटर, आरओ प्लांट ऑपरेटर, पंप ऑपरेटर
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "operator" → ask which machine or plant; "fireman" → ask boiler ka fireman (here) ya fire safety ka (Safety / EHS Officer); "chiller operator" or "AC plant operator" → Refrigeration & AC (HVAC) Technician; "substation operator" or "electrician" → Industrial Electrician; "plant operator" → ask utility plant hai ya production ka plant; "generator mechanic" or "pump mechanic" → Maintenance Technician
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `Utility Operator · {level} · {experience} · {utility_plant_type} · {utility_boiler_certificate} · {city}`

## T44 · Service Engineer (Field / Machine Service)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `service_engineer` |
| Department | Maintenance & Automation |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_service_engineer` |
| Profiling family | `fam_service_engineer` |
| Base match skill | `service_engineer` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 2144.0601 Service Engineer – Breakdown Service; 2144.1401 Service Engineer – Installation; 2144.1402 Service Engineer – Installation and Commissioning. Unit group(s): 2144 Mechanical Engineers |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): none. Apprenticeship: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. NSQF job roles: Service Engineer - Installation (CGSSC, CSC/Q0501); Service Engineer - Installation and Commissioning (CGSSC, CSC/Q0502); Service Engineer - Breakdown Service (CGSSC, CSC/Q0503). Also: Diploma / B.E. in Mechanical, Electrical, Electronics or Mechatronics; OEM product training (machine tool, compressor, DG, moulding-machine makers); Two-wheeler licence and willingness to travel are practical requirements |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹14,000–20,000 · Service Engineer ₹20,000–32,000 · Senior Engineer ₹32,000–50,000 · Area / Team Lead ₹48,000–75,000 · Manager ₹70,000–1,20,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Service Engineer `engineer` → Senior Engineer `senior_engineer` → Lead `lead` (shown as "Area / Team Lead") → Manager `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_engineer` |
| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |
| Related trades | Maintenance Engineer (T43); Maintenance Technician (T13); Project / Site Engineer (Mechanical) (T50); Technical Sales / Application Engineer (T51) |
| Typical employers | Machine tool makers and their dealers, moulding and packaging machine makers, compressor, DG and HVAC service dealers, material-handling and welding equipment companies, medical and lab equipment companies |

## T47 · Process / Manufacturing Engineer

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `process_engineer` |
| Department | Production & Engineering |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_process_engineer` |
| Profiling family | `fam_process_engineer` |
| Base match skill | `process_engineer` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 2141.0300 Industrial Engineer; 2141.0301 Industrial Engineer Executive; 2141.0600 Methods Engineer; 2144.0101 Process Design Engineer; 2144.0102 Process Try-Out Engineer; 2144.0103 Process Try-Out Technician; 2144.0104 Process Validation Executive; 2144.0105 Manager, Process Engineering; 2144.0300 Tool Engineer; 2144.1301 Layout Engineer; 2144.1302 Industrial Engineer (Workstation Design); 3115.0401 Method Study Executive; 3119.0700 Methods, Time and Motion Study Technician. Unit group(s): 2141 Industrial and Production Engineers; 2144 Mechanical Engineers; 3119 Physical and Engineering Science Technicians Not Elsewhere Classified; 3115 Mechanical Engineering Technicians |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): none. Apprenticeship: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. NSQF job roles: Automotive Production Equipment Design Engineer (ASDC, ASC/Q6405); Fundamentals of Low Cost Automation in Manufacturing Process (NOS-based course) (ASDC, ASC/N6464). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); B.E. / B.Tech Mechanical, Production or Industrial Engineering; Lean / Six Sigma, PFMEA and core-tools training, time-study (MOST) courses; Tooling background from MSME Tool Rooms / NTTF helps |
| Pay by rung | Trainee (GET / DET) ₹16,000–23,000 · Engineer ₹25,000–38,000 · Senior Engineer ₹38,000–60,000 · Lead / Asst. Manager ₹55,000–85,000 · Manager / HOD ₹80,000–1,40,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee (GET / DET) `trainee` → Engineer `engineer` → Senior Engineer `senior_engineer` → Lead / Asst. Manager `lead` → Manager / HOD `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_engineer` |
| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |
| Related trades | Production Engineer (T46); NPD / Development Engineer (T48) |
| Typical employers | Auto OEM and Tier-1 plants (ME / PE / IE departments), auto-component and engineering goods makers, appliance plants, new plant and line projects |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** process engineer, manufacturing engineer, me engineer, me department, mfg engineer, manufacturing engg, process engg, process enginer, industrial engineer, ie engineer, ie department, methods engineer, production engineering department, pe department, process planning engineer, tooling engineer, time study engineer, time study ka kaam, line balancing ka kaam, process sheet banata hoon, pfmea control plan banata hoon, layout planning engineer, lean engineer, cost reduction engineer, vave engineer, new line setup ka kaam
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** प्रोसेस इंजीनियर, मैन्युफैक्चरिंग इंजीनियर, एमई इंजीनियर, इंडस्ट्रियल इंजीनियर, आईई इंजीनियर, टूलिंग इंजीनियर, प्रोसेस प्लानिंग, टाइम स्टडी
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "process technician" → Injection Moulding Operator (senior rung); "process engineer" in a chemical or pharma plant → ask, chemical process work is not this trade; "production engineer" → Production Engineer; "fixture design" or "tool design" → 3D CAD Designer / Mechanical Design Engineer; "NPD" or "development engineer" → NPD / Development Engineer; "process quality" or "PQC" → Quality Engineer (QA / QC / SQA); "CNC programming" → CAM Programmer
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `Process / Manufacturing Engineer · {level} · {experience} · {proc_function} · {proc_process_type} · {city}`

## T49 · Safety / EHS Officer

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `safety_officer` |
| Department | Production & Engineering |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_safety_ehs` |
| Profiling family | `fam_safety_ehs` |
| Base match skill | `safety_officer` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 2141.2500 Safety Engineer; 2141.2600 Occupational Health and Safety Specialist; 2263.9900 Environmental and Occupational Health and Hygiene Professionals, Other; 3257.0600 Health, Safety and Environment Officer. Unit group(s): 2263 Environmental and Occupational Health and Hygiene Professionals; 3257 Environmental and Occupational Health Inspectors and Associates; 2141 Industrial and Production Engineers |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Fire Technology and Industrial Safety Management, 1 year, NSQF 3.5; Health, Safety & Environment, 1 year, NSQF 3. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named on the schedule read; optional-trade list was not readable this session. NSQF job roles: Safety Supervisor (OSHE) (Safety Skill Development Foundation); Industrial Safety Engineer (State Board of Technical Education & Training, Andhra Pradesh, submission id QG-5.5-HY-04443-2025-V1-SBTETAP (NQR code pending when read)). Also: Statutory route: degree or diploma in engineering / science plus a recognised diploma in industrial safety - ADIS (Advanced Diploma in Industrial Safety, state technical boards such as MSBTE) or PDIS (Post Diploma in Industrial Safety, Regional / Central Labour Institutes under DGFASLI and state boards) - required under state Factories Rules for a notified 'Safety Officer'; general knowledge, not re-verified this session; NEBOSH IGC and IOSH (valued by MNCs and for Gulf jobs; not a substitute for ADIS / PDIS in law); Diploma in Fire & Safety (many private one-year diplomas are not recognised for the statutory post); ISO 45001 / ISO 14001 lead auditor |
| Pay by rung | Safety Supervisor / Trainee ₹16,000–24,000 · Safety Officer ₹25,000–42,000 · Senior Safety Officer ₹40,000–60,000 · Lead / Asst. Manager EHS ₹55,000–85,000 · EHS Manager / HOD ₹80,000–1,40,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Safety Supervisor / Trainee `trainee` → Safety Officer `engineer` → Senior Safety Officer `senior_engineer` → Lead / Asst. Manager EHS `lead` → EHS Manager / HOD `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `engineer` |
| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |
| Related trades | None |
| Typical employers | Engineering, auto, chemical and pharma factories, construction and EPC project sites, power, steel and cement plants, safety manpower contractors |

## T50 · Project / Site Engineer (Mechanical)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `project_site_engineer` |
| Department | Production & Engineering |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_project_site` |
| Profiling family | `fam_project_site` |
| Base match skill | `project_site_engineer` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 8211.1000 Erector, Machine and Equipment. Unit group(s): 2144 Mechanical Engineers; 8211 Mechanical Machinery Assemblers. Partial gap: no Project Engineer or Site Engineer (Mechanical) title. File a new entry under 2144. 2144.1402 Service Engineer – Installation and Commissioning is given to Service Engineer (T44) only |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): none. Apprenticeship: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. NSQF job roles: Service Engineer - Installation and Commissioning (CGSSC, CSC/Q0502). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); B.E. / B.Tech Mechanical or Production; MS Project / Primavera courses; PMP for senior rungs; Site experience with an EPC or erection contractor counts most |
| Pay by rung | Trainee (GET / DET) ₹15,000–22,000 · Engineer ₹22,000–36,000 · Senior Engineer ₹35,000–55,000 · Site In-charge ₹50,000–80,000 · Manager ₹75,000–1,30,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee (GET / DET) `trainee` → Engineer `engineer` → Senior Engineer `senior_engineer` → Lead `lead` (shown as "Site In-charge") → Manager `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_engineer` |
| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |
| Related trades | Service Engineer (Field / Machine Service) (T44) |
| Typical employers | EPC and project companies, machinery and plant OEMs, erection contractors, HVAC / fire fighting / MEP contractors, PEB and material-handling system makers |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** site engineer, site engineer mechanical, mechanical site engineer, project engineer, project site engineer, site enginer, erection engineer, commissioning engineer, erection and commissioning, e&c engineer, installation engineer, site incharge, site in charge, site supervisor mechanical, erection supervisor, site execution engineer, project execution, piping site engineer, hvac site engineer, mep engineer, fire fighting site engineer, peb site engineer, structural erection engineer, plant erection ka kaam, site par erection karwata hoon, project manager mechanical
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** साइट इंजीनियर, प्रोजेक्ट इंजीनियर, इरेक्शन इंजीनियर, कमीशनिंग इंजीनियर, साइट इंचार्ज, इंस्टॉलेशन इंजीनियर, साइट सुपरवाइजर, प्रोजेक्ट मैनेजर
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "civil site engineer" or "civil supervisor" → civil construction work, not this trade; "site supervisor" → ask civil or mechanical; "service engineer" → Service Engineer (Field / Machine Service); "project engineer" in plant NPD → NPD / Development Engineer; "electrical site engineer" → ask, electrical project work is not this trade; "piping designer" → Piping Designer / Draughtsman; "site safety officer" → Safety / EHS Officer; "erector" or "site fabricator" doing the work by hand → Fabricator / Structural Fitter
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `Project / Site Engineer (Mechanical) · {level} · {experience} · {site_project_type} · {sector_worked} · {city}`

## T51 · Technical Sales / Application Engineer

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `technical_sales_engineer` |
| Department | Production & Engineering |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_technical_sales` |
| Profiling family | `fam_technical_sales` |
| Base match skill | `technical_sales_engineer` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 3322.0101 Sales Representative, Technical. Unit group(s): 2433 Technical and Medical Sales Professional (Excluding ICT); 3322 Commercial Sales Representatives. Partial gap: no Sales Engineer or Application Engineer title. File a new entry under 2433 |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): none. Apprenticeship: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); B.E. / B.Tech Mechanical or Production; MBA (Marketing) for regional-manager rungs; Product training by the principal (cutting tools, machine tools, CAD/CAM software, instruments); Two-wheeler / car and territory travel are practical requirements |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹15,000–22,000 · Sales Engineer ₹22,000–38,000 · Senior Engineer ₹36,000–58,000 · Area / Regional Manager ₹55,000–95,000 · Manager / Head ₹90,000–1,60,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Sales Engineer `engineer` → Senior Engineer `senior_engineer` → Lead `lead` (shown as "Area / Regional Manager") → Manager / Head `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_engineer` |
| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |

| Field | Value |
|---|---|

Apprenticeship: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. | Pay by rung | Trainee (GET / DET) ₹15,000–21,000 · PPC Executive / Engineer ₹21,000–33,000 · Senior Engineer ₹33,000–50,000 · Lead / Asst. Manager ₹48,000–75,000 · Manager / HOD ₹70,000–1,20,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |

| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |
| Related trades | Production Engineer (T46); Purchase / Vendor Development Engineer (T54) |
| Typical employers | Auto component plants (Tier-1 / Tier-2), OEM plants, machining, press, moulding, casting and forging units, appliance and engineering companies |

## T55 · Dispatch / Logistics Executive

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `dispatch_logistics_executive` |
| Department | Planning & Supply Chain |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_dispatch_logistics` |
| Profiling family | `fam_dispatch_logistics` |
| Base match skill | `dispatch_logistics_executive` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 1324.1200 Manager, Storage and Warehousing; 1324.1201 Warehouse Supervisor; 3323.0601 Associate – Supply Chain Management; 4321.0601 Warehouse Picker; 4321.0602 Warehouse Binner; 4321.0603 Warehouse Packer; 4321.0604 Kitting and Labelling Executive; 4323.9900 Transport Clerks, Other; 9321.0102 Packager and Despatcher. Unit group(s): 4321 Stock Clerks; 4323 Transport Clerks; 3331 Clearing and Forwarding Agents; 9321 Hand Packers; 1324 Supply, Distribution and Related Managers; 3323 Buyers. Partial gap: no Dispatch or Logistics Executive title. Vol I shows no occupations under 3331 Clearing and Forwarding Agents and only rail/tram titles under 4323. File a new entry under 4323 |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): In Plant Logistics Assistant, 1 year, NSQF 3.5; Warehouse Technician, 1 year, NSQF 3.5. Apprenticeship: No designated trade named on the schedule read; optional-trade list was not readable this session. NSQF job roles: Documentation Assistant (LSC, LSC/Q1122); Transport Coordinator (LSC, LSC/Q1118); Consignment Tracking Executive (LSC, LSC/Q1121); Automotive Packing Assistant (ASDC, ASC/Q6102). Also: Any graduate / 12th pass with experience; E-way bill, GST invoicing and customer-portal (ASN) practice learned on the job; Diploma in logistics / supply chain (private) |
| Pay by rung | Dispatch Helper / Assistant ₹13,500–16,500 · Dispatch Executive ₹16,000–25,000 · Senior Executive ₹24,000–34,000 · Dispatch / Logistics In-charge ₹30,000–50,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Dispatch Helper / Assistant `trainee` → Dispatch Executive `executive` → Senior Executive `senior_executive` → Dispatch / Logistics In-charge `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_executive` |
| Higher contact price from | `senior_executive` |
| Related trades | Store Keeper / Stores Executive (T53) |
| Typical employers | Auto component and engineering plants, FMCG and appliance factories, exporters, warehouses and 3PL companies, distribution centres |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** dispatch, dispatch executive, dispatch incharge, dispatch in charge, dispatch supervisor, dispatch assistant, dispatch ka kaam, despatch, dispach, dispetch, logistics executive, logistic executive, logistics ka kaam, logistics incharge, fg store, finished goods store, warehouse executive, warehouse incharge, billing aur dispatch, invoice banana, eway bill banana, asn banana, gaadi lagana, transport executive, transport ka kaam, export documentation, maal bhejna, loading supervisor
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** डिस्पैच, डिस्पैच का काम, डिस्पैच इंचार्ज, लॉजिस्टिक्स, वेयरहाउस इंचार्ज, माल भेजना, गाड़ी लोडिंग, बिलिंग और डिस्पैच
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "store keeper" / "raw material store" → Store Keeper / Stores Executive; "driver" / "gaadi chalata hoon" → not this trade, ask kaunsi gaadi; "forklift" → Forklift / Reach Truck Operator; "packing ka kaam" alone → ask haath ki packing (helper) ya dispatch ka kaagaz bhi; "billing" alone → ask accounts ki billing ya dispatch ka invoice; "delivery boy" / "courier" → not a factory trade, ask again; "dispatch planning" → PPC / Production Planning Engineer; "loading unloading" → helper unless he supervises loading
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `Dispatch / Logistics Executive · {level} · {experience} · {dispatch_function} · {dispatch_erp_system} · {city}`

## T57 · EOT Crane Operator / Rigger

| Field | Value |

Unit group(s): 8343 Crane, Hoist and Related Plant Operators; 7215 Riggers and Cable Splicers |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): none. NSQF job roles: EOT Overhead Crane Operator (IISSSC, ISC/Q0901); Rigger: Rigging of Heavy Material (IISSSC, ISC/Q0908); Junior Crane Operator (mobile crane) (Infrastructure Equipment Skill Council, IES/Q0111). | Higher contact price from | `senior` |
| Related trades | Forklift / Reach Truck Operator (T56) |
| Typical employers | Steel plants and rolling mills, foundries and forge shops, press and moulding shops, heavy fabrication and machine builders, power plants, ports, erection and construction contractors |
