# Department 11: Design
Live, untouched: T05 2D CAD Draughtsman.

## T58 · 3D CAD Designer / Mechanical Design Engineer

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `cad_designer_3d` |
| Department | Design |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | DEEPEN (supersedes the live thin form) |
| Form id | `qp_cad_design_3d` |
| Profiling family | `fam_cad_design_3d` |
| Base match skill | `cad_designer_3d` |
| NCO-2015 | Bind: 2144.0200 Designer, Machine; 2144.0201 Designer - Mechanical; 2144.0301 Equipment Designer; 2144.0800 Automobile Designer; 2144.0801 Product Design Manager; 2144.0803 Product Design Engineer; 2512.0203 Design Engineer(Product Engineer). Unit group(s): 2144 Mechanical Engineers; 2512 Software Developers |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Draughtsman Mechanical, 2 years, NSQF 4; Engineering Design Technician, 1 year, NSQF 3.5; Additive Manufacturing Technician (3D Printing), 1 year, NSQF 3.5. Apprenticeship: Designated trades: Draughtsman (Mechanical) (2 years); CAD-CAM Operator cum Programmer (1 year 3 months). Engineers: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. NSQF job roles: Computer Aided Product Design (NOS-based course) (ASDC, ASC/N8114); Electric Vehicle Product Design Engineer (ASDC, ASC/Q8104); Plastics Product & Mould Designer (CIPET, CPC/Q3104); Senior Tool Designer CAD / CAM (MSME Technology Centre, MSME/CSC/Q3704); Draughtsman - Mechanical (CGSSC, CSC/Q0402). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); B.E. / B.Tech Mechanical or Production; MSME Tool Room / NTTF / CIPET tool-design post-diplomas (most valued for press-tool and mould design); Private CAD institutes for SolidWorks, CATIA, NX, Creo (commonest route; certificate itself carries little weight); Vendor certificates (CSWA / CSWP) - general knowledge |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹14,000–20,000 · Designer ₹20,000–35,000 · Senior Designer ₹35,000–55,000 · Lead / Design Engineer ₹50,000–80,000 · Design Head ₹80,000–1,40,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Designer `designer` → Senior Designer `senior_designer` → Lead / Design Engineer `lead` → Design Head `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_designer` |
| Higher contact price from | `designer` |
| Related trades | 2D CAD Draughtsman (T05); NPD / Development Engineer (T48); CAE / FEA Analyst (T59) |
| Typical employers | Auto component makers, SPM and machine builders, tool rooms and mould makers, sheet-metal and fabrication companies, appliance and consumer-product companies, engineering-services and design outsourcing firms |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** 3d design, 3d designer, cad designer, cad engineer, design engineer, mechanical design engineer, design ka kaam, 3d modelling, 3d modeling, solidworks designer, solid works, catia designer, nx designer, ug nx, unigraphics, creo designer, pro e, inventor, fusion 360, product design, mould design, mold design, tool design, die design, press tool design, jig fixture design, sheet metal design, spm design, machine design, biw fixture design
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** 3डी डिज़ाइन, कैड डिज़ाइनर, डिज़ाइन इंजीनियर, सॉलिडवर्क्स, कैटिया, मोल्ड डिज़ाइन, टूल डिज़ाइन, जिग फिक्स्चर डिज़ाइन, शीट मेटल डिज़ाइन, मशीन डिज़ाइन
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "autocad" / "draughtsman" / "2d drawing" → 2D CAD Draughtsman; "design" alone → ask mechanical, civil / interior, electrical or graphic design; "cad cam" → ask design (here) or CAM programming (CAM Programmer); "mastercam" / "powermill" / "delcam" → CAM Programmer; "ansys" / "hypermesh" / "analysis" → CAE / FEA Analyst; "piping design" / "pdms" → Piping Designer / Draughtsman; "artcam" → CNC Router / Engraving Operator (ArtCAM); "npd" / "development engineer" → NPD / Development Engineer
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `3D CAD Designer · {level} · {experience} · {design_specialisation} · {design_software} · {city}`

1. **`design_experience`** (tier: easy; pick one; mandatory)
   - Asks: "3D design ke kaam ka kitna tajurba hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Tajurbe ke hisaab se aage ke sawaal poochhe jaate hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Fresher → `0`; 1 saal se kam → `0`; 1 se 3 saal → `2`; 3 se 5 saal → `4`; 5 se 10 saal → `7`; 10 saal se zyada → `12`

Unit group(s): 2144 Mechanical Engineers; 2512 Software Developers; 2152 Electronics Engineers. Partial gap: no title named CAE or FEA Analyst. The engineering-analysis titles that exist are filed by India under 2512 (Software Developers) and 2152. New entries should go under 2144 |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Virtual Analysis And Designer (Finite Element Method), 2 years, NSQF 4. Apprenticeship: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. Also: B.E. / B.Tech Mechanical, Automobile or Aerospace; M.Tech (Design / CAD-CAM) is common; Private CAE institutes for HyperMesh, ANSA, ANSYS, Abaqus, LS-DYNA; Vendor training (Altair, Ansys) |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹18,000–28,000 · Analyst ₹30,000–50,000 · Senior Analyst ₹50,000–85,000 · Lead ₹80,000–1,30,000 · Manager / Head ₹1,20,000–2,00,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Analyst `engineer` → Senior Analyst `senior_engineer` → Lead `lead` → Manager / Head `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_engineer` |
| Higher contact price from | `engineer` |
| Related trades | 3D CAD Designer / Mechanical Design Engineer (T58) |
| Typical employers | Auto OEM and Tier-1 R&D centres, engineering-services companies, CAE consulting and software reseller firms, tool rooms and foundries running moulding, casting or forming simulation |

**Words that lead here (Hinglish):** cae, cae engineer, cae analyst, fea, fea engineer, fea analyst, analysis engineer, simulation engineer, hypermesh, meshing, meshing engineer, ansys, abaqus, nastran, optistruct, ls dyna, ansa, cfd, cfd engineer, fluent, star ccm, moldflow, mould flow analysis, crash analysis, nvh, durability analysis, structural analysis, stress analysis, autoform, casting simulation
**Words that lead here (Devanagari):** सीएई इंजीनियर, एफईए एनालिस्ट, हाइपरमेश, एनसिस, मेशिंग, सिमुलेशन इंजीनियर, एनालिसिस इंजीनियर, सीएफडी
**Words that must NOT lead here:** "analyst" / "analysis" alone → ask FEA / CAE or data, lab or quality analysis; "testing" → ask simulation (here) or physical test lab (Material Testing / Met Lab Technician); "design engineer" → 3D CAD Designer / Mechanical Design Engineer; "caesar" / "pipe stress" → Piping Designer / Draughtsman; "cnc simulation" / "vericut" → CAM Programmer; "staad" / "etabs" → not this trade, civil structural design
**Chips that add a second match skill:** None
**Résumé headline:** `CAE / FEA Analyst · {level} · {experience} · {cae_domain} · {cae_solver} · {cae_preprocessor} · {city}`

## T60 · Piping Designer / Draughtsman

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Trade key | `piping_designer` |
| Department | Design |
| Kind | Desk |
| Status | NEW |
| Form id | `qp_piping_design` |
| Profiling family | `fam_piping_design` |
| Base match skill | `piping_designer` |
| NCO-2015 | No occupation in NCO-2015: a new catalogue entry is needed. Unit group(s): 3118 Draughtpersons; 2144 Mechanical Engineers. No piping designer or piping draughtsman occupation in NCO-2015. File under 3118 for draughtsmen and 2144 for designers. Nearest existing title is 3118.0201 Plumbing Draftsman (building plumbing, not process piping). A new catalogue entry is needed |
| Training route | ITI (CTS): Draughtsman Mechanical, 2 years, NSQF 4; Draughtsman (Civil), 2 years, NSQF 4 (adjacent). Apprenticeship: Designated trade: Draughtsman (Mechanical) (2 years). Engineers: Graduate / Technician (diploma) apprentice under NATS (Board of Apprenticeship Training) in the relevant engineering branch; no trade-level designated trade. NSQF job roles: Draughtsman - Piping (CGSSC, CSC/Q0403). Also: Diploma in Mechanical Engineering (polytechnic, 3 years); B.E. / B.Tech Mechanical or Chemical; Private piping-design institutes teaching PDMS / E3D, SP3D, AutoCAD Plant 3D, Caesar II (commonest route; quality varies); Experience at an EPC or detail-engineering office counts most |
| Pay by rung | Trainee ₹15,000–22,000 · Designer ₹25,000–45,000 · Senior Designer ₹45,000–75,000 · Lead ₹70,000–1,10,000 · Head ₹1,00,000–1,60,000 per month (ESTIMATE) |
| Ladder | Trainee `trainee` → Designer `designer` → Senior Designer `senior_designer` → Lead `lead` → Head `supervisor` |
| Confirmed from | `senior_designer` |
| Higher contact price from | `designer` |
| Related trades | 2D CAD Draughtsman (T05) |
| Typical employers | EPC companies, detail-engineering consultancies, engineering outsourcing and drafting firms, plant owners' project teams, skid, package unit and piping fabrication shops |

   - Chips (label → stored value): Carbon steel → `carbon_steel`; Stainless steel, SS304 ya SS316 → `stainless`; Alloy steel, P11, P22 ya P91 → `alloy_steel`; Duplex → `duplex`; HDPE, FRP ya GRP → `non_metallic`; Lined pipe, PTFE ya rubber lined → `lined_pipe`; Jacketed piping → `jacketed`; Pata nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 2 OR `piping_level` is one of senior_designer, lead, supervisor
13. **`piping_equipment_area`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Kaunse equipment ke aas-paas ki piping aapne ki hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Equipment piping ka tajurba layout ke kaam me sabse zyada dekha jaata hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Pump piping → `pump`; Column ya tower piping → `column`; Heat exchanger → `exchanger`; Pipe rack → `pipe_rack`; Vessel aur reactor → `vessel_reactor`; Compressor ya turbine → `compressor`; Tank farm → `tank_farm`; Utility station aur steam tracing → `utility_tracing`; Underground aur fire water piping → `underground`
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 2 OR `piping_level` is one of senior_designer, lead, supervisor
14. **`piping_support_work`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Pipe support ka kaunsa kaam aap karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Support ka kaam jaanne wale designer kam milte hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Standard support chunna, shoe, guide ya anchor → `standard_support`; Special support drawing banana → `special_support`; Support ki location aur span tay karna → `support_span`; Spring hanger ka data stress team se lena → `spring_support`; Support MTO → `support_mto`; Support ka kaam nahi kiya → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 2 OR `piping_level` is one of senior_designer, lead, supervisor
15. **`piping_site_exposure`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Site ya fabrication shop ka kaunsa kaam aapne kiya hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Site dekh chuke designer ki drawing zyada practical hoti hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Site par as-built marking → `as_built_markup`; Site survey karke modelling → `site_survey`; Fabrication shop ke liye spool drawing → `spool_shop`; Site office me construction support → `construction_support`; Hydrotest pack ya test loop banana → `test_pack`; Site ka tajurba nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 2 OR `piping_level` is one of senior_designer, lead, supervisor
16. **`piping_stress_exposure`** (tier: advanced; pick one)
   - Asks: "Stress-critical line par aapka kaam kitna hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Stress ka kaam jaanne wale designer ko senior kaam milta hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Stress ka kaam nahi kiya → `none`; Stress team ke comment model me lagata hoon → `incorporate_comments`; Caesar II me basic model aur run karta hoon → `caesar_basic`; Caesar II me poora analysis aur report banata hoon → `caesar_full`
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 5 OR `piping_level` is one of lead, supervisor
17. **`piping_checking_work`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "Checking aur coordination me aap kya kya karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Checking aur coordination karne wale ko lead banaya jaata hai."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Isometric checking → `iso_check`; GA aur layout checking → `ga_check`; Vendor drawing review, equipment nozzle → `vendor_drawing_review`; Civil, electrical aur instrument se interface → `discipline_interface`; Client ke saath model review → `client_model_review`; MTO reconcile karna → `mto_reconcile`; Site query ka jawab dena → `site_query`; Man-hour estimate aur progress report → `manhour_estimate`; Inme se koi nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 5 OR `piping_level` is one of lead, supervisor
18. **`piping_admin_work`** (tier: advanced; pick any)
   - Asks: "3D software ke admin ka kaunsa kaam aap karte hain?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Admin ka kaam jaanne wale log bahut kam milte hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Piping spec aur catalogue banana → `catalogue_spec`; Isometric ya drawing template setup → `template_setup`; Project database setup → `project_setup`; Report ya MTO customise karna → `report_customise`; PML ya macro likhna → `pml`; Admin ka kaam nahi karta → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at least 5 OR `piping_level` is one of lead, supervisor
19. **`piping_qualification_branch`** (tier: medium; pick one; core)
   - Asks: "Aapki padhai kis branch ya course se hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Kai engineering company branch dekhkar hi bulati hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Diploma Mechanical → `diploma_mech`; B.Tech / BE Mechanical → `btech_mech`; Chemical engineering → `chemical_engg`; Production ya Industrial engineering → `production_engg`; ITI Draughtsman, Mechanical ya Civil → `iti_draughtsman`; ITI ke baad promote hua → `iti_promoted`; B.Sc ya koi aur degree → `other_degree`; Sirf private institute ka piping course → `piping_course`
20. **`piping_training_exposure`** (tier: easy; pick any; core)
   - Asks: "Padhai ke dauraan piping ka kaunsa practical exposure mila hai?"
   - Reason told to the worker: "Training ka exposure bhi tajurba hai, employer ise dekhte hain."
   - Chips (label → stored value): Internship company me → `internship`; Apprenticeship (NATS / NAPS) → `apprenticeship`; College ka major project → `college_project`; Private institute ka piping ya PDMS course → `piping_course`; Plant ya site visit hi hua hai → `industrial_visit`; Kuch nahi → `unknown` (escape chip)
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at most 0
21. **`iti_project_work`** (tier: medium; free text)
   - Asks: "College ya training me kya project banaya tha? Apne shabdon me bataiye."
   - Reason told to the worker: "Banaya hua project dikhata hai ki aap kya kar sakte hain."
   - Asked only when: `piping_experience` is at most 0

## 11. What to send back

Only these. Everything else is ruled.

1. **Campus role names not in section 7.2.** One list of the role names in real use that have no row. They will be ruled in the same table.
2. **Key clashes in the 7 deepened forms.** For T17, T24, T27, T29, T38, T45 and T58: any live question key that asks the same thing as a question here under a different key. Keep the live key; tell us which.
3. **Occupation codes.** Section 13 gives the exact codes. Send back only: any code there that is missing from your catalogue, any code your system already holds under another family, and the new entries you created for CMM, NDT and Piping.
4. **Sign-off names.** For each sheet, the practising worker or trainer who read it, and the date. Chips changed by him are final; send the changed lines.
5. **Anything the loader rejects,** with the sheet code and question number.
6. **Three numbers after wave 1 is loaded** (re-run of your section 6.1): share of Campus jobs with a trade, share of RVM students with a trade, share of RVM courses that reach their own trade's jobs.

## 12. Sources, method and confidence

**Method.** Trades were listed from four directions and then merged: (1) the departments of an Indian auto-component or engineering plant, from goods-in to dispatch; (2) official occupation and training lists: NCO-2015 unit groups, DGT's Craftsmen Training Scheme trades, NSQF job roles of the manufacturing Sector Skill Councils; (3) what Campus already sees (the role counts in section 6.1 of your brief); (4) the trades already live. A role became a trade only when workers identify with it by name and a company hires for it by name; otherwise it was folded in as a chip or a rung (section 10).

**Checks run on every sheet (by script):** question-key format, one question mark, at most 20 words, no banned words, six fixed experience bands first, ladder second with stored values equal to the ladder keys, a separating question for every rung from the confirmed rung up, 2 to 9 chips with one escape chip at most, every condition pointing at an earlier question and at real chip values, a fresher block, 18 to 23 questions.

**Confidence, honestly.**

| Part | Confidence | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Trade list and department structure | High | Cross-checked against official lists and the Campus data |
| Ladders, confirmed and higher-price rungs | High for machine trades; medium for desk trades | Desk ladders follow common Indian plant titles; small firms use fewer rungs |
| Questions and chips | Medium to high | Written to the live forms' pattern and machine-checked for form. **Not yet read by a practising worker of each trade** (section 11, item 4) |
| Worker words (Hinglish and Devanagari) | Medium | Good starting set; the unresolved-phrase queue will add the rest in the first weeks |
| NCO-2015 codes | High | Every 8-digit code was read in the official NCO-2015 Volume I code table. 57 of 60 trades have codes; 3 have none in the classification (section 13.2). Occupation descriptions (Volume II) were not read, so a few generic titles need a look (section 13.3) |
| ITI and NSQF routes | High for ITI trades; medium for the rest | ITI names, durations and levels were read on DGT's trade list. Apprenticeship durations come from an undated schedule copy. NSQF job-role codes were seen on official pages for 51 trades, some from a 2018-19 list that may be superseded. Lines under "Also" are general industry knowledge |
| Size, demand and pay | Low to medium | MODELED and ESTIMATE. Use for ordering work only |

**Three cautions on pay.** (1) One range covers three regions, but the legal floor differs: skilled minimum wage is about ₹18,500 in Haryana, ₹15,800 in Pune and ₹14,200 in Ahmedabad, and higher again in Delhi proper. Do not show a fresher salary in the app without the city. (2) 23 pay ladders (EDM, gear, boring, SPM, router, tube bending, foundry, die casting, forging, heat treatment, plating, all four plastics and rubber trades, CMM, NDT, calibration, material lab, NPD, PPC, CAE, CNC grinding) rest on a neighbouring job title plus the wage floor. (3) Rung names in the pay column for the 16 live trades follow this file's index, not necessarily your live labels.

**Lines the authors were least sure of** (the sign-off reader should look here first): machine and instrument brand chips in T17, T23, T33, T35 and T42; the shop-floor words "garari" (T18), "farana" (T24), "jasta chadhana" (T28) and "khalasi" (T57); the product chips in T30 (water tank, preform); the material chip `bearing_steel` in T27 against the live `alloy_steel`; the qualification question in T36 and T49.

**Sources read (23 Nov 2026).**

- NCO-2015 Volume I, Directorate General of Employment: https://dge.gov.in/dge/sites/default/files/2022-07/National_Classification_of_Occupations_Vol_I-2015.pdf
- Craftsmen Training Scheme trade list (170 trades), Directorate General of Training: https://www.dgt.gov.in/en/cts-details
- Schedule of designated apprenticeship trades (DGT regional directorate copy, undated)
- Qualification packs and National Qualification Register entries: ASDC, CGSSC, CIPET, nqr.gov.in, AICTE's 2018-19 PMKVY job-role list
- Minimum-wage notifications for Haryana, Delhi, Maharashtra, Gujarat and Uttar Pradesh, as carried on compliance sites (not the gazettes)
- Pay spot checks: Indeed India, PayScale India, AmbitionBox and Apna listing pages for Pune, Ahmedabad and Faridabad
- Annual Survey of Industries 2024-25 press note (MoSPI); Business Today 27 Aug 2026; Autocar Professional 2 Sep 2026 (BCG-ACMA report); TeamLease manufacturing staffing release, Dec 2024
- Your brief of 23 Nov 2026 (live trades, ladders, match skills, form rules, Campus counts)

### 13.2 Gaps: what the classification does not have

Three trades have no occupation at all and need a new catalogue entry. The others have codes, but one part of the trade is not named.

| Code | Trade | Gap and what to do |
|---|---|---|
| T33 | CMM Operator / Programmer | **No occupation.** No CMM operator or programmer occupation in NCO-2015. File under 7543 (nearest existing titles: 7543.2001 Quality Inspector - Forged, Casted or Machined Components; 7543.3002 Quality Control Inspector - Dimension Check). A new catalogue entry is needed. |
| T34 | NDT Technician | **No occupation.** No NDT, industrial radiography or ultrasonic testing occupation in NCO-2015. File under 7543 (testers) for operators, or 3119 for Level II/III technicians. A new catalogue entry is needed. Do not use 3211.0100 X-Ray Technician: it is medical. |
| T60 | Piping Designer / Draughtsman | **No occupation.** No piping designer or piping draughtsman occupation in NCO-2015. File under 3118 for draughtsmen and 2144 for designers. Nearest existing title is 3118.0201 Plumbing Draftsman (building plumbing, not process piping). A new catalogue entry is needed. |
| T01 | CNC Turner | The three generic CNC operator titles (7223.5001, 7223.5002, 7223.5003) name no single machine. Leave them with whichever live family holds them today and do not bind them to a second one; a worker arriving on them gets the routing question in section 6. |
| T02 | VMC / Milling Operator | The three generic CNC operator titles (7223.5001, 7223.5002, 7223.5003) name no single machine. Leave them with whichever live family holds them today and do not bind them to a second one; a worker arriving on them gets the routing question in section 6. |
| T03 | CNC Grinding Operator | Partial gap: NCO-2015 has no CNC, cylindrical, centreless or internal grinding title. Only conventional grinder titles exist, and India files them under 7224, not 7223. A new catalogue entry (CNC Grinding Machine Operator) is needed; file it under 7224 next to the existing grinder titles. |
| T04 | CAM Programmer | Partial gap: no CAM-specific title. CNC Programmer (7223.6003, filed by India under 7223) is the only programming title. Do not use 8189.0301 CAM Machine Operator: it belongs to Gem and Jewellery Machine Operators. |
| T09 | Sheet Metal Worker | Partial gap: no title for laser cutting, CNC press brake or turret punch operator. Nearest existing titles are 7223.2400 Sheet Metal Machine Operator and 7223.2000 Bending Machine Operator. New entries should go under 7223. |
| T11 | Painter / Powder Coater | Partial gap: no powder coating title in NCO-2015. File a new entry under 7132. |
| T17 | EDM / Wire-cut Operator | Partial gap: wire-cut EDM is not named. The only EDM title is 7223.2800 (die-sinking EDM). A new entry for Wire-cut EDM Operator is needed under 7223. |
| T20 | Honing / Broaching / SPM Operator | Partial gap: no broaching, gun drilling or special purpose machine operator title. New entries should go under 7223. |
| T21 | CNC Router / Engraving Operator (ArtCAM) | Partial gap: no CNC router, acrylic or ArtCAM designer title. Nearest existing titles are the two wood-working machine titles bound here. File a new entry under 7523 (wood) and, for stone, under 8112. |
| T23 | Tube Bending / Roll Forming Operator | Partial gap: no tube bending or roll forming title. 7223.2000 Bending Machine Operator is the only bending title. A new entry is needed under 7223. 7223.2000 Bending Machine Operator is given to this trade only. If the live Sheet Metal family already holds it, leave it there and add a new catalogue entry for tube bending. |
| T24 | Foundry / Casting Operator | Partial gap: no metal fettler, pourer or induction-furnace melter title (8114.0700 Fettler is brick and tile; the 3135 furnace titles are smelting and open-hearth). File new entries under 7211 or 8121. |
| T28 | Plating / Surface Treatment Operator | Partial gap: anodising and phosphating are not named; Surface Treatment Technician (8122.0101) is the nearest. |
| T31 | Plastic Extrusion Operator | Partial gap: pipe, film, cable and compounding are not separately named. |
| T38 | PLC / Automation Technician | Partial gap: no PLC programmer or automation technician title. 7412.0101 Automation Specialist is the only automation title. File new entries under 3113 or 3139. Do not use 8212.2002 PLC Assembly Operator (assembles PLC hardware). |
| T39 | Robot Operator / Programmer | Partial gap: no robot programmer title; file a new entry under 3139. |
| T50 | Project / Site Engineer (Mechanical) | Partial gap: no Project Engineer or Site Engineer (Mechanical) title. File a new entry under 2144. 2144.1402 Service Engineer – Installation and Commissioning is given to Service Engineer (T44) only. |
| T51 | Technical Sales / Application Engineer | Partial gap: no Sales Engineer or Application Engineer title. File a new entry under 2433. |
| T55 | Dispatch / Logistics Executive | Partial gap: no Dispatch or Logistics Executive title. Vol I shows no occupations under 3331 Clearing and Forwarding Agents and only rail/tram titles under 4323. File a new entry under 4323. |
| T59 | CAE / FEA Analyst | Partial gap: no title named CAE or FEA Analyst. The engineering-analysis titles that exist are filed by India under 2512 (Software Developers) and 2152. New entries should go under 2144. |

### 13.3 Rules for binding

1. **One code, one family.** Five codes fitted two trades each; they are already resolved above (the three generic CNC operator titles, 7223.2000 Bending Machine Operator, 2144.1402 Service Engineer – Installation and Commissioning). If your live system already holds any code here under another family, the live binding wins; tell us.
2. **If a code is missing from your catalogue, drop that binding.** Do not create an occupation from this file, except the three new entries in 13.2.
3. **Do not import codes from skill-council qualification files.** Two of three files checked carried old NCO-2004 codes under an NCO-2015 label.
4. **India files some occupations in unexpected families.** Grinding, honing and lapping sit under 7224, not 7223. CNC Programmer is 7223.6003. Press Shop Operator is under 7211. Quality Control Inspector is under 3139. Inspection, Safety and Instrumentation Engineer are under 2141. The CAE titles are under 2512 (software developers). A trade bound only by unit group would miss these.
5. **8189 is the jewellery family.** "CAM Machine Operator" (8189.0301) and "Pantograph Machine Operator" (8189.0701) must not be bound to CAM Programmer or to CNC Router.
6. **Check the description before binding these generic-titled codes**, which were bound on wording alone: 7543.0701, 7543.0702, 7543.3002, 7543.3004, 7543.3005, 8142.0502, 3113.0401, 3113.0601. If your catalogue shows a narrow other-sector meaning, unbind.
7. **Left unbound on purpose:** 2144.0100 Mechanical Engineer, General; 3118.0301 Draftsman; 2166.0401 Designer (CAD); 2144.0802 Modeller; and every automotive-service, textile, food, telecom and jewellery title. They are too generic or belong elsewhere.
8. **Official spellings are kept as printed** ("Mechanist, General/Machinist", "Jig and Fixture Marker"), so the strings match your catalogue.
